stdout | packages/cli/src/tui/terminal-ui.test.ts > TerminalUI > showHelp() > should display available commands

Available Commands:

Conversation and sessions
  /new [title]                 Create a conversation
  /list [search]               List or filter conversations
  /switch <id|number>          Switch conversation
  /name <title>                Rename the active conversation
  /delete <id>                 Delete a conversation
  /clear                       Clear the active conversation
  /session [-a|--all]          Show session statistics
  /search <text>               Search the transcript
  /copy                        Copy the last assistant response

Providers and context
  /provider [name]              List or select a provider
  /model [list|cycle|name]      List or select a model
  /thinking [level]             Set off/minimal/low/medium/high/xhigh/max
  /login <provider> [--issuer github-copilot|openai-codex] [--method browser]  Start an explicit OAuth device flow
  /compact [instructions]       Compact the active conversation
  /summarize-branch [id] [text] Store a branch summary
  /fork <id> [index] [title]    Fork a conversation
  /clone <id> [title]           Clone a conversation
  /tree [branch-id]             Show the session tree

Files, resources and sharing
  /export <format> [path]        Export json/markdown/jsonl/html
  /import <path>                Import JSON or JSONL sessions
  /share [hours]                Create a temporary share link
  /bug [description]            Prepare a GitHub issue URL
  Privacy: review exports, public shares and diagnostic text for unrecognized secrets.
  /skills                       List discovered skills
  /skill:<name> [request]       Invoke a skill
  /prompts                      List prompt templates
  /<prompt> [arguments]         Expand a prompt template

Extensions and application
  /extensions                   List loaded extensions
  /mcp [status|tools|exposure|test] Inspect/manage MCP (status is passive)
  MCP: missing exposure = direct; codemode is script-only; deferred needs tool_search.
  Hidden is not model-callable; allowlists, exclusions and read-only checks still apply.
  /tools                        List built-in and extension tools
  /tool <name> [json]           Run a registered tool
  /trust [status|allow|allow-parent|refuse|forget|list] Manage project trust decisions
  /reload                       Reload settings, resources and extensions
  /settings                     Show effective agent settings and sources
  /config                       Show storage configuration
  /help                         Show this help
  /quit or /exit                  Exit AiHarness

Input
  /edit / /send / /cancel          Multi-line input
  !<command>                    Run a shell command and include its output in context
  !!<command>                   Run a shell command outside model context
  Enter                         Send now, or steer an active response
  Alt+Enter / Ctrl+Q             Queue a follow-up after the active task
  Alt+Up                        Restore queued messages to the editor
  Alt+J                         Insert a newline; Enter submits the whole draft
  Ctrl+Left/Right / Alt+B/F      Move by whitespace-delimited word
  Alt+Backspace / Alt+D          Delete previous/next word
  Ctrl+Z / Alt+Z                Undo/redo draft edits (bounded, not submitted messages)
  Bracketed paste is inert until Enter (64 KiB maximum); unbracketed input is ordinary typing.
  Native terminal selection only; no application mouse, inline images or custom terminal component protocol.
  Stored unsupported preferences are reported by /settings, not silently applied.
  Ctrl+G                        Open $VISUAL or $EDITOR
  Escape / Ctrl+C               Interrupt; queued messages return to the editor
  F1 / Alt+K                     Browse/filter all keyboard actions; Escape returns

Providers:
  mock, openai, anthropic, google, local, azure, vertex, bedrock

Main environment variables:
  OPENAI_API_KEY, ANTHROPIC_API_KEY, GEMINI_API_KEY
  LOCAL_BASE_URL / LLAMA_BASE_URL, AI_HARNESS_AGENT_DIR
  AI_HARNESS_SESSIONS_DIR, AI_HARNESS_TUI_MODE, AI_HARNESS_KEYBINDINGS
